BOARD OF COMMISSIONERS OF UTAH COUNTY, UTAH
Public Meeting Minutes

December 9, 2020

1liage

PUBLIC NOTICE IS HEREBY GIVEN THAT THE
BOARD OF COMMISSIONERS OF UTAH COUNTY, UTAH

WILL HOLD A PUBLIC MEETING
IN THE COMMISSIONER CHAMBERS — ROOM 1400
OF THE UTAH COUNTY ADMINSTRATION BUILDING
100 East Center Street, Provo, Utah
December 9, 2020 — 3:00 P.M.

*Board members may participate electronically at will, with the anchor location as stated above.*

*In accordance with the High Transmission Area restrictions instituted by the Department of
Health in Utah County, members of the public are strongly encouraged to participate
electronically. To accommodate physical distancing requirements, physical attendance will be
limited to 20 people (including staff) at the anchor location. Once the room has reached
capacity the doors will be locked, and additional attendees will be invited to participate online.
All individuals in attendance must comply with State Public Health Order 2020-19 requiring
face coverings in indoor spaces where social distancing is not possible, with certain
exemptions*

*The public may participate electronically by joining via zoom at the following link
https://zoom.us/j/9938818466 or calling (346) 248-7799 or (669)900-6833, Meeting ID: 993 881
8466. Comments will be limited to three (3) minutes per individual unless otherwise approved by the
Board. Please state your name at the beginning of the comment. Please no foul or abusive language.*

*If a member of the public is unable to provide comment electronically then they may contact the
commission clerk to submit written comments for the record by emailing

commissionclerk@utahcounty.gov or by calling 801-851-8111*

~COMMISSION MEETING MINUTES~

Commissioner Lee and Commissioner Ivie in attendance
Commissioner Ainge (Chair) via Zoom

(Meeting called to order: 3:03 P.M.)

PRAYER/READING/THOUGHT: Member of Audience
PLEDGE OF ALLEGIANCE: Member of Audience

COMMISSIONER LEE — THIS WAS QUITE A PROCESS. I SPENT MANY, MANY DAYS
IN INTERVIEWS WITH QUALIFIED CANDIDATES ACROSS THE COUNTRY AND
ERIC EDWARDS ROSE TO THE TOP OF THAT LIST. HE’S VERY, VERY QUALIFIED
TO TAKE OVER THIS POSITION AND SO HE IS THE ONE THAT WAS SUBMITTED TO
US FROM THE BOARD OF HEALTH FOR THE POSITION OF EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR
OF THE UTAH COUNTY BOARD OF HEALTH.

ERIC EDWARDS, EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR, HEALTH — IT’S HUMBLING, BUT ALSO,
I’M EXCITED FOR THE OPPORTUNITY TO ASSUME THIS ROLE AND OF ALL TIMES
TO DO THIS, DURING THE MIDDLE OF A PUBLIC HEALTH PANDEMIC - IT IS
QUITE REMARKABLE. YES, IT’S QUITE THE OPPORTUNITY AND I’M EXCITED FOR
IT. MY CHARGE IS TO PRESERVE AND PROTECT THE HEALTH OF THE PUBLIC
AND THAT’S WHAT WE’RE HERE FOR AS A HEALTH DEPARTMENT IS TO
ACHIEVE THAT ORGANIZATIONAL MISSION. THAT’S MY CHARGE AND ’PM
EXCITED FOR THE OPPORTUNITY AND I THANK YOU FOR PROVIDING ME THIS
OPPORTUNITY TO SERVE THE PUBLIC RESIDENTS HERE IN UTAH COUNTY.

COMMISSIONER LEE —- THANK YOU. LOOK FORWARD TO SERVING WITH YOU.
COMMISSIONER IVIE —- CONGRATULATIONS ERIC.

COMMISSIONER AINGE - THANK YOU. WE APPRECIATE YOUR INVOLVEMENT IN
THE PROCESS, YOUR WILLINGNESS TO STEP UP AND I THINK IT ALSO GOES
WITHOUT SAYING, THAT WE’RE GRATEFUL FOR THE SERVICE OF OUR
OUTGOING HEALTH DIRECTOR, RALPH CLEGG IN HIS INCREDIBLY LONG AND
DEDICATED CAREER OF APPROXIMATELY 38 YEARS.

COMMISSIONER AINGE - I RECEIVED A LETTER JUST A SHORT TIME AGO, BUT I
HAD RECEIVED A LOT OF COMMUNICATION FROM DIFFERENT MEMBERS OF
THE COMMUNITY. I’LL START BY READING THIS LETTER. IT CAME FROM
MAYOR WESTMORELAND OF EAGLE MOUNTAIN. ON A NOTE; ONLY 2 OF THE 22
MAYORS DID NOT SUPPORT PUBLICLY THE CHANGE ON PROPOSITION 9 AND
MAYOR WESTMORELAND WAS ONE OF THOSE AND I THINK YOU'LL SEE IN THE
SPIRIT OF THESE COMMENTS AND THE INTRODUCTION I’M ABOUT TO MAKE
THAT THE POINT IS REGARDLESS OF WHERE YOU WERE ON THAT TOPIC,
THERE’S A LOT OF CONSENSUS ABOUT SOME CHANGES THAT WE COULD MAKE
TO MAKE COUNTY GOVERNMENT MORE EFFECTIVE, MORE EFFICIENT. THIS IS
THE LETTER.

“DEAR UTAH COUNTY COMMISSIONERS, THIS PAST ELECTION VOTERS
REJECTED A WHOLESALE CHANGE TO UTAH COUNTY’S FORM OF
GOVERNMENT, SO WE WILL CONTINUE WITH THE 3-COMMISSIONER
STRUCTURE. STILL THE ISSUE OF EFFECTIVE GOVERNANCE REMAINS AS WE
TRY TO PROVIDE THE BEST COUNTY GOVERNMENT AT THE LOWEST COST. A
CHANGE WOULD BE A STEP IN THIS DIRECTION WOULD BE TO SWITCH FROM
DEDICATED PERSONAL ASSISTANTS FOR EACH COMMISSIONER TO A SHARED
SUPPORT MODEL. ONLY TWO UTAH COUNITES USE DEDICATED RESOURCES
HIRED BY EACH ELECTED OFFICIAL —- UTAH AND SALT LAKE COUNTY. THE
REMAINDER OF COUNTIES ALL USE A SHARED SUPPORT MODEL (AND THEN HE
SAYS WHERE). SOME OF THEIR ADVANTAGES THEY SAY IS RETENTION OF
INSTITUTIONAL KNOWLEDGE BECAUSE YOU DON’T HAVE AUTOMATIC
TURNOVER WITH NEW ELECTIONS, STAFF ARE VIEWED AS NEUTRAL ON
POLITICAL ISSUES. THEY DON’T CARRY THE SAME BAGGAGE AS SOMEONE
WHO’S DEDICATED OR ALIGNED WITH EACH OF THE ELECTED OFFICIALS AND
BECAUSE THEY REPRESENT THE ENTIRE COMMISSION, ADMINISTRATIVE TASKS
CAN BE DELEGATED TO THIS BODY ALLOWING COMMISSIONERS TO FOCUS ON
BROADER POLICY AND ADMINISTRATIVE FUNCTIONS. WE BELIEVE THAT
SWITCHING TO A SHARED SUPPORT STAFF CAN BE MORE COST EFFICIENT AND
PROMOTE A LESS DIVISIVE ENVIRONMENT AT THE COUNTY.”

I’M NOT GOING TO READ THE REST OF IT BUT AT THE END HE STATES THAT
WITH A NEW COMMISSIONER COMING INTO OFFICE IN JANUARY, THE SOONER

BOARD OF COMMISSIONERS OF UTAH COUNTY, UTAH
Public Meeting Minutes

December 9, 2020

Blrage

THE COMMISSION ADDRESSES THIS ISSUE, THE LESS DISRUPTION THERE WILL
BE AMONG EXISTING EMPLOYEES.

I THINK THAT’S INTERESTING COMING FROM THE COMMUNITY. IN MY OWN
OPINION, WE STILL DO NEED TO LOOK AT WAYS THAT WE CAN MAKE COUNTY
OPERATIONS MORE EFFECTIVE AND IN WAYS THAT WILL LEAD TO BETTER
GOVERNANCE. WHEN I LOOK PAST THE PROP 9 DISCUSSION AND INTO THE
COMING YEAR WITH COMMISSIONER LEE AND COMMISSIONER-ELECT
SAKIEVICH, I THOUGHT ABOUT HOW WE CAN CONTINUE TO COLLABORATE,
TOM AND I RAN AGAINST EACH OTHER IN THE SAME PRIMARY IN 2018 AND
DURING THAT TIME, WE BOTH TALKED ABOUT TWEAKS THAT COULD BE MADE.
GEOGRAPHIC DISTRICTING WAS SOMETHING WE BOTH SPOKE ABOUT AND
THEN IN 2019 HE AND COMMISSIONER LEE WERE SPONSORS OF A PLAN TO
MOVE TO A 5-MEMBER COMMISSION WITH 5 GEOGRAPHIC DISTRICTS AND
MOVING TO A % TIME COMMITMENT.

I WAS READING SOME OF THIS THAT WHEN THE DAILY HERALD ASKED
COMMISSIONER LEE ABOUT THE PART-TIME FEATURE, COMMISSIONER LEE
SAID, “THIS ALLOWS FOR SOMEBODY TO COME ON AND STILL KEEP THEIR
CAREER PATH GOING BUT STILL TO BE FULLY INVOLVED I SEE IT AS
INCREASING OUR BASE OF INDIVIDUALS WHO WOULD LIKE TO LOOK AT BEING
A PUBIC SERVANT.” I WHOLEHEARTEDLY AGREE ON THAT TOPIC. RIGHT NOW,
WE CAN’T TAKE ANY ACTION AT THE COUNTY LEVEL TO MOVE TO FIVE. THAT
REQUIRES SOME STATE COORDINATION, BUT I’M WILLING TO TAKE A SERIOUS
LOOK AT THESE REFORMS WITH COMMISSIONER LEE AND COMMISSIONER
SAKIEVICH.

I WENT TO HIGH SCHOOL IN MARICOPA COUNTY, ARIZONA, AND THEY HAVE
THIS MODEL. THEY HAVE A 5-MEMBER MODEL GEOGRAPHIC DISTRICTS.
THEY’RE BASICALLY A % TIME COMMISSION BOARD OF SUPERVISORS AND I
SPOKE WITH THEM AND ASKED, “HOW DO YOU DO THAT? HOW DOES IT NOT
FEEL LIKE YOU HAVE 5 CEO’S TRYING TO DO VERY ADMINISTRATIVE TASK?”
THEY RESPONDED SAYING THAT IT WORKS WELL FOR THEM BECAUSE THEY
HAVE A STRONG ADMINISTRATOR AND THEY WERE BAFFLED TO HEAR THAT
WE DID NOT HAVE THAT TYPE OF FUNCTION. SO, AS I LOOK TO EXPLORE THIS
MODEL, I THINK THERE’S A LOT OF BENEFITS TO IT EVEN WITHOUT THAT, BUT I
DO THINK IT’S IMPORTANT THAT WE LOOK AT THIS SHARED STAFF AS THE
MAYOR SAID OR ADMINISTRATIVE FUNCTION.

SO ON TO WHAT THIS AGENDA ITEMS ACTUALLY IS, IS THAT’S WHAT IT DOES —
IT CREATES AN ADMINISTRATOR POSITION. I’ VE GONE BACK AND FORTH ON
THE TITLE. I THINK ON THE AGENDA ITEM IT SAYS, ‘CHIEF ADMINISTRATIVE
OFFICER’. YOU COULD ALSO CALL IT A ‘DIRECTOR OF ADMINISTRATION AND
PUBLIC AFFAIRS’. THAT SPEAKS TO 2 OF THE MAIN ISSUES THAT YOU
COMMONLY HEAR WITH PEOPLE WHO DEAL WITH COUNTY GOVERNMENT IS: 1)
SOMEONE NEEDS TO HAVE THAT CENTRALIZED TASK TO TAKE CARE OF THOSE
SMALL THINGS AND 2) ON THE PUBLIC AFFAIRS SIDE, IT’S REALLY HARD TO
UNDERSTAND COMMUNICATIONS FROM THE COUNTY WHEN IT COMES FROM 3
DIFFERENT VOICES AND FOR SOME BASIC THINGS, WE DO NEED AT LEAST A

THIS IS THE PROPOSAL. ON THE BUDGETARY PERSPECTIVE, ALL OF THE

COMMISSIONER IVIE - FOR ME, I ACTUALLY HAVE SOME INITIAL CONCERNS
ABOUT THE CHIEF ADMINISTRATIVE OFFICER POSITION. I ALWAYS GET
CONCERNED HAVING A NON-ELECTED INDIVIDUAL SERVING AS A CHIEF
ADMINISTRATOR SO THAT’S ALWAYS A CONCERN FOR ME WHEN IT COMES TO
GOVERNMENT JUST ON A PRINCIPLED MATTER. THE REASON IT TROUBLES ME
IS BECAUSE I CAN THINK OF A HANDFUL OF PEOPLE WHO I WOULD
ABSOLUTELY LOVE TO SEE IN THAT POSITION AND A HANDFUL OF PEOPLE WHO
I WOULD DREAD TO SEE IN THAT POSITION AND SO THAT GIVE ME PAUSE AS TO
IF THIS IS REALLY A GOOD MODEL TO GO WITH. IF IT’S HIGHLY DEPENDENT
UPON THAT SIMPLY BECAUSE AS A SOON-TO-BE ONLY CITIZEN, I CAN’T VOTE
FOR THAT PERSON. IF I VOTE FOR THEM AND THEY WIN, GREAT. IF I VOTE FOR
THEM AND THEY LOSE, THAT’S GREAT TOO BECAUSE I STILL GOT THE
OPPORTUNITY TO VOTE FOR THE PERSON WHO WOULD BE HANDLING THE
VAST MAJORITY OF THE ADMINISTRATIVE OFFICES SO THAT ONE ON A
PRINCIPLED MATTERS DOES GIVE ME PAUSE AND CONCERN.

I WOULD SAY ON THE SECOND PORTION OF THIS, I ACTUALLY REALLY LIKED
SOME OF THE CONTEXT AND AS WAS PRESENTED IN THE WORK SESSION ON
CENTRALIZING SOME OF THE COMMISSION STAFF. I THINK THAT TAKES A LOT
OF THE POLITICALNESS OUT OF IT, HELPS THE COMMISSION WORK BETTER AS
A WHOLE UNIT AND THESE PEOPLE ARE SIMPLY DEDICATED TO THE COUNTY
AS OPPOSED TO ONE INDIVIDUAL. SO, ON THE SECOND PART OF THIS
PROPOSAL, THERE’S COMPONENTS TO THAT THAT I REALLY LIKE
STRUCTURALLY. MY CONCERN WITH THAT IS I WOULD PERSONALLY WANT TO
HAVE A CONVERSATION WITH COMMISSIONER-ELECT SAKIEVICH TO GET HIS
FEELINGS ON THAT BECAUSE I DON’T NECESSARILY WANT TO THRUST
SOMETHING ON HIM JUST BECAUSE I THINK IT’S A GOOD IDEA, BUT I’D BE
HAPPY TO SHARE THAT WITH HIM. I’M PAUSED BECAUSE OF THE

AMELIA POWERS GARDNER, UTAH COUNTY CLERK/AUDITOR - THE TENTATIVE
BUDGET WAS ADOPTED IN OCTOBER. THE TENTATIVE BUDGET FOR 2021 WAS
$106,719,661. SINCE THAT TIME, WE’ VE WORKED WITH SEVERAL OF THE
DEPARTMENT HEADS AND SOME OF THEM WITHDREW SOME REQUESTS,
BRINGING THAT DOWN SOME. ON TOP OF THAT, THE OUTLOOK FOR THE
ECONOMY IN 2021 HAS BECOME A LOT SOFTER THAN IT WAS WHEN WE
ORIGINALLY CREATED THE TENTATIVE BUDGET, SO OUR EXPECTED REVENUES
HAVE COME DOWN SLIGHTLY.

MY DEPARTMENT DID SOME REORGANIZATION AND WE’VE BEEN TALKING
ABOUT THIS FOR YEARS AND WE TALKED ABOUT IT MORE RECENTLY WITH
YOU COMMISSIONER LEE AND ALSO COMMISSIONER IVIE AND AINGE THAT WE
WANTED TO RESTRUCTURE THE WAY THAT WE DO ACCOUNTING A LITTLE BIT.
THE MAIN REASON FOR THAT AND ESPECIALLY FOR THE FOLKS LISTENING TO
HELP BETTER UNDERSTAND THAT, THERE’S A LOT OF PROGRAMS
THROUGHOUT THE COUNTY THAT ARE FUNDED BY STATE AND FEDERAL
GRANTS. THEY COME WITH OUTSIDE FUNDS AND THOSE PROGRAMS ARE
SUPPLEMENTED BY OUR TAXPAYERS AND BY OUR PROPERTY TAXES IN THE
WAY THAT ALL OF THE HUMAN RESOURCE COSTS FOR THE PEOPLE WHO WORK
IN THOSE DIVISIONS COME DIRECTLY OUT OF OUR GENERAL FUND AS WELL AS
ANY OF THE PURCHASING THAT IS DONE FOR THEM. ANY OF THE ACCOUNTS
PAYABLE OR ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE OR ANY OF THE CIVIL LITIGATION FROM
THE ATTORNEY’S OFFICE. ALL OF THOSE THINGS GO TO SUPPORT THESE
FUNCTIONS, EVEN IF THESE ARE GRANT FUNDED DIVISIONS. WHEN WE DO
THAT, WE’RE SUPPLEMENTING THOSE PROGRAMS. THEY’RE REALLY
PRESENTED TO THE PUBLIC AS BEING GRANT FUNDED BUT THEY’ RE BEING
SUPPLEMENTED USING OUR TAXPAYER DOLLARS. SO, IF WE REARRANGE THE
WAY THAT WE DO OUR ACCOUNTING AND ACCOUNT FOR THOSE THINGS, WHAT
IT DOES IS IT TAKES SOME OF THOSE OVERHEAD COSTS LIKE H.R. MY AUDITOR
FUNCTIONS, INTERNAL AUDIT, ACCOUNTS PAYABLE, ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE,
PURCHASING, THE CIVIL DIVISION OF THE ATTORNEY’S OFFICE, AND PUTS
THEM INTO WHAT’S CALLED AN ADMINISTRATIVE FUND AND THEN EACH
DEPARTMENT PAYS THAT ADMINISTRATIVE FUND BASED OFF OF THEIR SIZE.
THAT MORE ACCURATELY ACCOUNTS FOR THOSE THINGS. WE WERE GOING TO
WAIT UNTIL THE END OF 2021 TO REARRANGE THE WAY WE DO ACCOUNTING IN
OUR COUNTY SO THAT THAT WOULD BE ACCOUNTED FOR IN THE 2022 BUDGET.
BUT WHEN WE REALIZED THAT THE ECONOMIC OUTCOME AND THE OUTLOOK
FOR 2021 IS A LOT SOFTER THAN ECONOMISTS THOUGHT IT WOULD BE, THAT
WOULD HAVE GIVEN US ABOUT A $5 MILLION SHORTFALL WITH THE ADJUSTED
REVENUE NUMBERS. THAT’S A BIG SHORTFALL FOR US AND SO WE WENT
AHEAD AND IMPLEMENTED INTO THIS BUDGET, THAT CHANGE IN ACCOUNTING.
THAT WILL MAKE US MORE EFFICIENT AND MORE EFFECTIVE AS A COUNTY
AND IT WILL MORE ACCURATELY ACCOUNT FOR THE COST OF INDIVIDUAL
PROGRAMS THROUGHOUT THE COUNTY. DOING THAT SAVED US ABOUT $4
MILLION OUT OF THE GENERAL FUND BECAUSE THAT $4 MILLION IS THEN
REALLOCATED INTO OTHER REVENUE SOURCES AND OTHER FUNDS FOR OTHER
DEPARTMENTS. THAT’S REALLY THE BIG DIFFERENCE ON THIS BUDGET.

THEN THERE WERE A COUPLE OF THINGS ON THE BUDGET THAT ALL 3
COMMISSIONERS AGREED UNANIMOUSLY THAT THEY WEREN’T GOING TO

Byrany

IN THE 1960S, A PRIVATE ENTITY BUILT ON THE LAND UP THERE AND BUILT THE
TRAMWAY THAT WENT ON TO THE TOP OF THE FALLS, HAD HAD A
RESTAURANT AT THE TOP. IN 1996 THE LOWER SECTION OF THAT TRAM, THE
LOWER TRAM BUILDING WAS DESTROYED BY AN AVALANCHE. IN 2008 THE
BUILDING AT THE TOP, THE OLD RESTAURANT, WAS DESTROYED BY FIRE. AND
IN THE LATTER PART OF 2014, THE LANDOWNERS WERE TRYING TO SELL THE
PROPERTY. THEY HAD SOME INTERESTED PARTIES. THERE HAD BEEN A
CONDITIONAL USE PERMIT, I THINK IS THE RIGHT TERM ROB, IS THAT RIGHT?
(AFFIRMATIVE) AND THAT WAS APPROVED IN THE 1990S THAT ALLOWED FOR
COMMERCIAL AND RESIDENTIAL DEVELOPMENT IN AND AROUND THE AREA BY
BRIDAL VEIL FALLS. NOW THE COUNTY COMMISSION AT THE TIME SAID I
THINK WE OUGHT TO BUY THIS, AND AT THAT POINT I WAS DIRECTED TO WORK
WITH THE PROPERTY OWNERS AND THE COUNTY DID ULTIMATELY PURCHASE
THE PROPERTY IN 2015. IN 2017 BECAUSE OF THE OLD TRAM SHACK THAT HAD
BEEN PARTIALLY DESTROYED BY AN AVALANCHE AND THE REMAINDER OF THE
BUILDING ON THE TOP THAT WAS THE OLD RESTAURANT, THE COUNTY
CONTRACTED WITH A CONSTRUCTION FIRM TO DO THAT REMOVAL AND SPENT
ABOUT $1,000,000 TO REMOVE THOSE TWO STRUCTURES AND DO SOME OTHER
CLEANUP WORK AROUND THE FALLS. THERE WAS AN OLD BRIDGE STRUCTURE
THAT WAS THERE, IT WAS A VERY UNIQUE DEMOLITION PROJECT WHEN
YOU'RE WORKING IN THE TOP OF 1100 FOOT CLIFF. EVERYTHING HAD TO BE
FLOWN OUT BY HELICOPTER. ALSO, IN 2017 WE WERE DIRECTED BY THE
COUNTY COMMISSION TO DO A SURVEY AND TRY TO GET USER INPUT. AND WE
DID THAT AND COMMISSIONER LEE KIND OF ALLUDED TO IT IN HIS COMMENTS.
WE DIDN'T GET A WHOLE LOT OF RESPONSE, WE HAD 50 OR 60, BUT NOT A
GREAT DEAL OF RESPONSE FROM THE PUBLIC BUT THAT THEY DID ALL, THE
MAJORITY OF THOSE COMMENTS WERE THAT THEY WANTED TO SEE THE
FALLS STAY OPEN SPACE AND HAVE SOME IMPROVEMENTS TO ENHANCE THE
FALLS THEMSELVES BUT NOT MAJOR IMPROVEMENTS UP THERE THAT WOULD
BE IN THE LINE OF LIKE COMMERCIAL DEVELOPMENT AND THAT SORT OF
THING. IN 2018 WE DID A REQUEST FOR PROPOSALS TO HAVE CONCESSIONAIRE
GIVE US SOME PROPOSALS ON WHAT THEY COULD DO IN AND AROUND THE
AREA, WHAT IMPROVEMENTS COULD BE DONE. THE COMMISSION REVIEWED
THOSE AND ULTIMATELY DID NOT SELECT ANY OF THOSE. AND THEN IN 2019
THE MAJORITY OF WHAT'S ON THE SCREEN NOW IN THE CONCEPT PLAN WAS
APPROVED BY THE COUNTY COMMISSION. I'LL JUST POINT OUT TO YOU A
COUPLE OF THINGS THAT ARE ON THERE. THIS DOES ENHANCE SOME OF THE
PARKING AND TAKES SOME OF THE OLD PARKING LOT OUT THAT'S
IMMEDIATELY ACROSS FROM THE FALLS, PUTS THAT INTO A GREENSPACE
WHERE PEOPLE CAN COME AND ENJOY THE VIEW OF THE FALLS WITHOUT
STANDING IN THE PARKING LOT. THAT'S ON THE ROAD SIDE OF THE RIVER. IT
ALSO DOES SOME IMPROVEMENTS ON THE TRAIL TO GIVE AN OPPORTUNITY
FOR THE WHEELED TRAFFIC ON THE TRAIL TO BE OVER ON THE ROADSIDE AND
NOT DOWN THROUGH WHERE ALL THE PEOPLE ARE STANDING ON THE TRAIL

PUBLIC ABOUT A POSSIBLE CONSERVATION EASEMENT FOR BRIDAL VEIL FALLS
AND OTHER POTENTIAL USES FOR THAT AREA. AS THE PROVO MUNICIPAL
COUNCIL AND AS OUR PROVO CITY MAYOR, WE SHARE THIS LETTER TO OFFER
OUR PERSPECTIVE ON THIS IMPORTANT QUESTION. WE ARE UNITED IN OUR
DESIRE TO SEE THIS AREA PROTECTED FROM COMMERCIAL DEVELOPMENT.
WE SUPPORT ANY MEASURES TO PRESERVE AND ENHANCE THE SITE FOR THE
BENEFIT OF RESIDENTS AND VISITORS. BRIDAL VEIL FALLS IS ONE OF THE
MOST SIGNIFICANT NATURAL WONDERS IN THE VALLEY. IT IS AN ICONIC
LOCATION OF REMARKABLE BEAUTY THAT ATTRACTS PEOPLE ALL YEAR
ROUND. AS ONE OF THE MOST POPULAR DESTINATIONS ON THE PROVO RIVER
PKWY TRAIL, IT’S AN INDISPENSABLE PLACE OF ENJOYMENT THAT PROVIDES
RECREATION AND ATHLETIC PLEASURE AND SPIRITUAL RENEWAL FOR
FAMILIES AND INDIVIDUALS AND TO THE YOUNG AND OLD ALIKE. IN ITS
CURRENT UNDEVELOPED STATE, THE AREA ATTRACTS PEOPLE PRECISELY
BECAUSE IT IS EXPERIENCED AS A NATURAL TREASURE, OWNED BY NO ONE BUT
PROTECTED AND PRESERVED FOR AND BY THE PUBLIC IN OUR VIEW,
ALLOWING DEVELOPMENT AND PRIVATIZATION TO COMPROMISE THE
PUBLICS’ EXPERIENCE OF THE AREA OF BRIDAL VEIL FALLS WOULD NOT SERVE
THE INTEREST OF THE PUBLIC CITIZENS AND VISITORS. TO PRIVATIZE AND
DEVELOP ANY PART OF THE FALLS WOULD BE A SIGNIFICANT DEPARTURE IN
OUR VIEW FROM OUR VISION OF NATURAL PRESERVATION FOR THIS AREA.
PLEASE GIVE SERIOUS CONSIDERATION TO TAKING APPROPRIATE ACTION TO
PREVENTING PRIVATE DEVELOPMENT THAT COULD COMPROMISE THE
NATURAL BEAUTY OF BRIDAL VEIL FALLS. SINCERELY, MAYOR MICHELLE
KAUFUSI AND THE PROVO MUNICIPAL COUNCIL. THANK YOU.

CONTRARY. OVER THE PAST WEEK, I'VE BEEN INVOLVED IN STARTING
MULTIPLE PETITIONS THAT CLEARLY DEMONSTRATE THE PUBLIC OPINION ON
THE TOPIC. THESE PETITIONS STARTED MERE DAYS AGO AND ALREADY SHOW
OVER 10,000 PEOPLE IN DIRECT OPPOSITION TO ALTERING FALLS THROUGH
DEVELOPMENT. GIVEN MORE TIME THAT NUMBER AND ATTENTION AROUND
THE TOP WILL INCREASE, SOLELY BASED ON ATTENTION SURROUNDING THE
PETITION, IT IS CLEAR WHERE THE PUBLIC IS ON THE ISSUE. ADDITIONALLY,
SOCIAL MEDIA ATTENTION SURROUNDING THE DEBATE OVER BRIDAL VEIL
FALLS FUTURE HAS INCREASED EXPONENTIALLY OVER THE PAST WEEK, WITH
TRAFFIC AND IMPRESSIONS ON THE TOPIC SHOWING HIGH ANALYTICS
INCREASING. SENTIMENT OF SOCIAL MEDIA POSTINGS INCREASED BY OVER
230% BETWEEN NEWS SURROUNDING THE FATE OF BRIDAL VEIL FALLS AND
THE RELEASE OF VARIOUS PETITIONS AGAINST THE ACTION, WITH THE HIGH
MAJORITY OF SENTIMENT BEING CLEARLY NEGATIVE. SHEER VOLUME OF
POSTING ON THE TOPIC THROUGH HASHTAGS OR POST COPY HAS INCREASED
BY OVER 205% IN THE PAST WEEK, WITH OVER 2000 MENTIONS FROM 1255
UNIQUE AUTHORS AND THE TOP URL LINKS AND SHARES HAVE GONE DIRECTLY
TO NEWS ARTICLES AND ANTI-REMOVAL PETITIONS. BRIDAL VEIL FALLS IS
MORE THAN A SCENIC LOCATION. IT IS A JEWEL THAT SIGNIFIES THE BEAUTY
AND OPPORTUNITIES THAT SET UTAH COUNTY APART FROM OTHER COUNTIES.

BEAUTIFUL LAND CAN BE PROTECTED FOREVER FROM DEVELOPMENT. THE
CONSERVATION EASEMENT DOESN'T RESTRICT PUBLIC USE, IT DOESN'T
RESTRICT IMPROVEMENT OF TRAILS, TRAILHEADS, RESTROOMS OR PAVILIONS.
THE COUNTY WILL STILL OWN LAND, THE EASEMENT WILL ONLY PREVENT
DEVELOPMENT FROM HAPPENING. IT WILL PROTECT THE LAND, THE ANIMALS,
AND PUBLIC USE. THE NORMAL COST THAT UTAH OPEN LANDS’ CHARGES TO
STEWARD A PARCEL OF LAND IS A ONE-TIME FEE OF $6500. | UNDERSTAND
THERE'S ADDITIONAL COST THAT THE COUNTY WOULD HAVE TO DO FOR
SURVEYING AND STUFF. I JUST WANT THE PUBLIC TO REALIZE THIS IS NOT A
HUGE COST FOR THE COUNTY. THIS FEE WILL PROTECT THE LAND AND ENSURE

AND TOURISM DESTINATIONS WITH THE SHORT DRIVE FROM THE HUSTLE AND

RECREATIONAL ACTIVITIES AND PERSONAL WELL-BEING, A PLACE TO WITNESS
NATURE AND WILDLIFE IN ITS GLORY. WITHOUT A THIRD-PARTY
CONSERVATION EASEMENT, A FUTURE UTAH COUNTY COUNCIL CAN VOTE IN

WITH YOUNG KIDS. THE BIKE PATH THAT RUNS PAST THE FALLS IS ALSO A MAJOR
ASSET TO THE COMMUNITY THERE. HAVING PREVIOUSLY LIVED IN VIVIAN PARK I
HAVE GREAT MEMORIES OF GOING RUNNING AND BIKING ON THE PATH AND
STOPPING TO ADMIRE THE FALLS. BRIDAL VEIL FALLS ALSO SERVES AS ONE OF
UTAH’S BEST ICE CLIMBING AREAS.

THERE ARE OTHER BEAUTIFUL AREAS THAT A REHAB CENTER COULD BE
LOCATED- WE SHOULD BE PROTECTING THE FALLS FOR THE COMMUNITY
(INCLUDING ICE CLIMBERS) TO ENJOY FOR GENERATIONS TO COME.

CRAIG STEVENS, CONCERNED CITIZEN OF SUMMIT COUNTY - PLEASE, PLEASE,
PLEASE PROTECT BRIDAL VEIL FALLS. DON'T LET THIS BEAUTIFUL NATURAL SPOT
BE TARNISHED BY ASAID PLAN FOR A FACILITY BUILT THERE. THERE IS PLENTY OF

ITS NATURAL LANDSCAPE FOR CLIMBING AND EXPLORING. IT IS WILD AND FREE
AND BELONGS THAT WAY.

B4[P one

SEAN TROPSA - UTAH COUNTY CLERK AND UTAH COUNTY COMMISSIONERS LEE,
AINGE, IVIE AND SAKIEVICH,

THE BRIDAL VEIL FALLS SPACE OF PROVO CANYON IS A BELOVED NATURAL AREA
WITH ONE OF THE MOST ACCESSED WATERFALLS IN THE US. IAM SURE YOU ALL
HAVE HEARD QUITE A FEW COMMENTS ALREADY, BUT I WOULD LIKE TO ADD ONE
MORE COMMENT ABOUT PRESERVING THE SPACE IN ITS NATURAL STATE.

NOW THAT WINTER IS HERE, WE CAN SEE THE PLETHORA OF ICE ROUTES FORMING
IN THE BRIDAL VEIL FALLS AREA. THE AREA IS THE CORNERSTONE OF ICE
CLIMBING IN THE SOUTHERN WASATCH AND ATTRACTS VISITORS FROM ACROSS
THE US TO COME AND CLIMB ON ITS FROZEN WATERFALLS. HISTORICALLY, THIS
AREA BAS BEEN PIVOTAL FOR THE DEVELOPMENT OF ICE CLIMBING WITH
NOTABLE HISTORIC ICE CLIMBERS VISITING THE AREA, CLIMBING ON THE FROZEN
WATERFALLS, AND USING THIS AREA TO DEVELOP NEW EQUIPMENT AND
TECHNIQUES THAT ARE STILL IN USE TODAY. AS AN ICE CLIMBER, I FREQUENT THE
AREA IN WINTER AS IT IS A GREAT TRAINING GROUND FOR ICE CLIMBING. THE
LOSS OF THIS SPACE TO A PRIVATE DEVELOPER WOULD BE AN UNQUANTIFIABLE
LOSS TO THE ICE CLIMBING COMMUNITY IN UTAH. ON TOP OF THAT, THE
PREVIOUS STRUCTURES THAT HAVE INHABITED THIS SPACE HAVE BEEN DAMAGED
DUE TO THE EXTREMELY DESTRUCTIVE AVALANCHE PATH THAT RESIDES ABOVE
THIS SPACE. DEVELOPING THIS AREA WOULD LIKELY RESULT IN FURTHER
PROPERTY DESTRUCTION AND POSSIBLE LOSS OF LIFE IF IT WAS INHABITED WHEN
A LARGE AVALANCHE COMES THROUGH.

AS A SUMMER USER OF THE PROVO RIVER THROUGH THIS STRETCH, I HAVE ALSO
SEEN HOW BELOVED THE WATERFALL IS TO SUMMER USERS. MANY THROUGHOUT
THE UTAH VALLEY AREA AND EVEN THOSE FROM SALT LAKE AND ELSEWHERE
TRAVEL TO THIS AREA TO ENJOY ITS UNIQUE BEAUTY, WHICH WAS ONLY
RECENTLY SECURED USING FUNDING FROM UTAH COUNTY. THIS SUMMER,
PARTICULARLY, THE AREA WAS A GO-TO FOR MANY DURING THE CORONA VIRUS
AS IT WAS A BEAUTIFUL SPACE OUTDOORS WHERE USERS COULD RECREATE IN A
MANNER SO AS TO COMPLY WITH CDC GUIDELINES AND LIMIT THE SPREAD OF THE
VIRUS. TO SELL THIS LAND, ESPECIALLY FOR THE LOW PRICE THAT HAS BEEN
SUGGESTED FOR IT (MUCH LOWER FROM NUMBERS THAT HAVE BEEN REPORTED,
THAN IT WAS PURCHASED FOR BY THE COUNTY OVER A DECADE AGO) WOULD BE A
HUGE LOSS FOR EVERYONE IN THE COUNTY AND IN NORTHERN UTAH AND WOULD
FAVOR A SMALL FEW WHO WOULD DEVELOP AND PROFIT OFF OF THE AREA AT
THE DETRIMENT OF THE WIDER CITIZENSHIP WHO CONTRIBUTED TAX DOLLARS
TO PURCHASE THE SPACE.

I URGE ALL OF YOU TO HAVE THE CITIZENS OF UTAH COUNTY AND NORTHERN
UTAH IN MIND AND VOTE TO KEEP THIS AREA PRESERVED IN ITS NATURAL STATE
FOR THE USE OF ALL UTAHNS, NOT ONLY THOSE WHO CAN PAY TO ACCESS THE
AREA ON A VERY LIMITED BASIS. PLEASE LISTEN TO THE USERS WHO FREQUENT
THIS AREA AND AID IN ITS PROTECTION AND PRESERVATION FOR FUTURE
GENERATIONS.

TYLER SMITHSON - TO WHOM IT MAY CONCERN, PLEASE PRESERVE BRIDAL VEIL
FALLS AS A NATURAL OPEN SPACE. IMPROVEMENTS NEEDED TO

CONTROL EROSION CAUSED BY MANY UNSUSTAINABLE SOCIAL TRAILS AND
PHOTO-OPPORTUNITY LOCATIONS.

OVER THE YEARS WE HAVE BEEN APPROACHED BY POTENTIAL DEVELOPERS, AND
THOUGH WE APPRECIATE IMPROVEMENTS IN THE CANYON, WE HONESTLY DO NOT
BELIEVE ANY DEVELOPER COULD IMPROVE ON THE FALLS AND WE FIND THEIR
CLAIMS OTHERWISE TO BE SELF-SERVING AND PRIMARILY IN THEIR OWN
INTERESTS.

WHILE THERE WILL ALWAYS BE SOME BENEFIT TO SOME SUBGROUP OF PEOPLE
THAT THEY CAN POINT TO IN THEIR PLANS, THE FACT REMAINS THAT THE
OVERWHELMING BENEFIT TO THE PUBLIC IS MET BY KEEPING THE FALLS IN
PUBLIC HANDS.

WHEN THE ORIGINAL GONDOLA AND GIFT CENTER WERE BUILT BACK IN THE 19608,
DEVELOPERS DID NOT HAVE TO PREPARE ENVIRONMENTAL IMPACT STATEMENTS.
THE CONDITIONS TODAY ARE MUCH DIFFERENT. THE POTENTIAL DEVELOPERS, AS
FAR AS WE KNOW, HAVE NOT PROPOSED OR DISCUSSED THE IMPACT A
RESORT/GONDOLA WITH ITS ATTENDANT TRAFFIC WOULD HAVE ON THE CANYON,
THE WATER, WILDLIFE, FIRE HAZARDS, AND THE NATURAL BEAUTY OF THE AREA.
THIS ADDS ALL THE MORE REASON TO HAVE THE CONSERVANCY EASEMENT IN
PLACE, TO PRESERVE THE BEAUTY OF THE FALLS AREA FOR ALL TO APPRECIATE.

WE BELIEVE THAT YOU ARE ACTING IN THE INTEREST OF THE ENTIRE COMMUNITY
BY ESTABLISHING THE EASEMENT. THE CURRENT PROCESS IS IN NO-WAY HASTY;
ON THE CONTRARY, WE BELIEVE THAT THE DECISION SHOULD HAVE BEEN MADE
YEARS AGO TO PREVENT THE TYPES OF DISCUSSION THAT ARE CURRENTLY
UNDERWAY.

WHETHER OR NOT THERE IS THE POTENTIAL FOR CURRENT OR INCOMING
COMMISSIONERS TO BE SWAYED BY DEVELOPERS, IT IS INEVITABLE THAT SOME
FUTURE SET OF COMMISSIONERS WILL TAKE A SHORT-TERM VIEW THAT IS NOT IN
THE BEST INTEREST OF THE PUBLIC. WE TRUST THAT THE CURRENT
COMMISSIONERS WILL DO MORE THAN PROTECT THE FALLS FROM DEVELOPMENT
ON A CASE-BY-CASE BASIS, BUT THAT THEY WILL PROTECT IT IN PERPETUITY FOR
US AND OUR CHILDREN.

1 AM ONE OF THOUSANDS WHO ENJOY THIS PART OF THE COUNTY REGULARLY.
PLEASE DO NOT SELL THIS FOR DEVELOPMENT. ANSWER TO YOUR VOTERS AND

IF THIS IS AN ISSUE OF NEEDING TO RAISE MONEY FOR THE COUNTY, THERE ARE

THOSE THINGS THAT STRENGTHEN COMMUNITIES AND INDIVIDUALS. I BELIEVE

OUR OFFICIALS HAD THE FORESIGHT TO STEP IN AND TEAR THE MESS WHICH WAS
LEFT BEHIND OFF THE MOUNTAIN.

FINANCIAL MARKETS, AVALANCHES, POOR MANAGEMENT ETC. ARE REALITIES
WHICH NO DEVELOPER CAN GUARANTEE AGAINST. THE FALLS SIMPLY IS NOT
SAFER NOR WILL IT BE IN BETTER HANDS, IF IT IS SOLD OR LEASED TO PRIVATE
ENTERPRISE. THE ONLY PREDICTABLE OUTCOME IN PRIVATIZING THE FALLS IS
RESTRICTED AND UNCERTAIN ACCESS AND THE CHANCE TO LIVE THE LAST 60
YEARS ALL OVER AGAIN. LET’S LEARN FROM THE PAST.

BRIDAL VEIL BELONGS TO THE PEOPLE. IT MUST STAY IN THE POSSESSION OF THE
PEOPLE. IT'S PART OF OUR HERITAGE AND IT'S PART OF US. IT IS AS IMPORTANT AS
MT TIMPANOGOS, ROCK CANYON, CASCADE MOUNTAIN, AND OUR, SOMETIMES
SPARKLING, SOMETIMES SWAMPY UTAH LAKE. THE COUNTY BOUGHT IT AND NOW
IT'S A PART OF OUR BIRTHRIGHT.

UTAH COUNTY COMMISSIONERS. OUR VALLEY IS EXPLODING WITH GROWTH.
PLACES OF NATURAL BEAUTY ARE ONLY BECOMING MORE SCARCE AND LESS
ACCESSIBLE. BRIDAL VEIL IS A UNIQUE ASSET WHICH IS ONLY INCREASING IN
VALUE TO THE PUBLIC YOU SERVE. YOUR JOB IS TO REPRESENT THE PEOPLE AND
TO WORK IN THEIR INTEREST AS WELL AS THAT OF FUTURE GENERATIONS.

WHEN UTAH COUNTY BOUGHT BRIDAL VEIL, IT WAS A STEP IN THE RIGHT
DIRECTION. DON'T GIVE UP NOW COMMISSIONERS. PRESERVE OUR FALLS. DO NOT
SELL IT. DO NOT LEASE IT. DO NOT LET US DOWN.

FOR SEVENTY HARDENING SEASONS I’VE WATCHED

THE STOPPING OF WATERFALLS. SOME OF THE TIME

I KNEW AND PERHAPS UNDERSTOOD HOW WATER
CHANGED IN WINTER, WHAT HAPPENED TO MOLECULES,

IN A NIGHT FROM BOUNDING LIQUID TO

AN OBDURATE SMOOTHNESS. NOT ANY LONGER.

ALL THAT’S CONFUSING NOW. I AM CONTENT

TO WATCH THE WORLD TURN COLD WITH ITS OLD GRACE.

GATHER FOR A FEW MINUTES OF SPLASHING IN THE FALLS. WE NEED A PLACE

ADMIRE THE BEAUTY. WE HAVE SUCH A PLACE IN BRIDAL VEIL FALLS. PLEASE
DON'T TRADE IT FOR A FEW SILVER COINS.

DAVID P. CARTER - MY NAME IS DAVID CARTER, AND I AM A MEMBER OF THE
BOARD OF DIRECTORS OF THE SALT LAKE CLIMBERS ALLIANCE (SLCA) AND CHAIR
THE SLCA POLICY AND CONSERVATION COMMITTEE. THE SLCA IS A LOCAL 501(C)(3)
NONPROFIT ESTABLISHED IN 2002. OUR MISSION IS TO SERVE AS THE UNIFIED
VOICE OF THE OVER 20,000 CLIMBERS IN THE GREATER WASATCH REGION,
ENGAGING AS AN ADVOCATE TO PROTECT OUTDOOR CLIMBING ACCESS AND AS A
STEWARD TO MAINTAIN SUSTAINABLE CLIMBING RESOURCES IN THE WASATCH
AND SURROUNDING REGIONS.

I WRITE TO YOU TODAY TO URGE THAT YOU ESTABLISH PERPETUAL
CONSERVATION AND PUBLIC ACCESS TO PROVO CANYON’S BRIDAL VEIL FALLS.

BRIDAL VEIL FALLS IS A CORNERSTONE OF UTAH ICE CLIMBING HISTORY — WHICH,
IN TURN, PLAYS A PROMINENT ROLE IN THE DEVELOPMENT OF ICE CLIMBING IN
THE GREATER U.S. AMONG THE EARLIEST RECORDED ASCENTS ARE GREG LOWE’S
ASCENT OF BRIDAL VEIL IN 1971. THE AREA DREW PROMINENT CLIMBERS, BOTH
LOCALLY AND NATIONALLY, THROUGHOUT THE LATE 1970S AND EARLY 80S. THE
BRIDAL VEIL AREA REMAINS A PROMINENT AND HEAVILY FREQUENTED ICE
CLIMBING DESTINATION TODAY.

WE ALSO RECOGNIZE THE RECREATIONAL AND AESTHETIC SIGNIFICANCE OF
BRIDAL VEIL FALLS FOR MANY MORE THAN ICE CLIMBERS. I CANNOT SAY HOW
MANY PEDESTRIANS, PHOTOGRAPHERS, AND OTHER VISITORS I HAVE WITNESSED
ON THE COLDEST DAYS (WELL BELOW ZERO DEGREES FAHRENHEIT) — MUCH LESS
WARMER DAYS AND MORE FAVORABLE CONDITIONS.

FOR THESE REASONS, I (AND THE GREATER SLCA) WOULD LIKE TO SEE LONG TERM
ACCESS MAINTAINED WHILE PROTECTING THIS NATURAL EXPERIENCE. WE
SUPPORT A CONSERVATION EASEMENT THAT ALLOWS FOR RECREATION AT
BRIDAL VEIL FALLS AND WE HOPE TO BE A STAKEHOLDER TO HELP STEWARD THIS
RESOURCE INTO THE FUTURE.

THANK YOU FOR HEARING THE SCLA AND CLIMBERS’ INTERESTS IN THE FUTURE
OF BRIDAL VEIL FALLS,

UTAH COUNTY IS HOME TO SEVERAL WORLD CLASS CLIMBING OPPORTUNITIES,
FROM SPORT CLIMBING IN AMERICAN FORK AND ROCK CANYON, TO ICE CLIMBING
IN PROVO CANYON.

OUR GROUP IS INTERESTED IN ESTABLISHING LONG-TERM PROTECTIONS FOR ONE
OF THE WASATCH FRONT’S MOST VALUABLE ICE CLIMBING RESOURCES AT BRIDAL
VEIL FALLS.

THE SLCA HAS A LONG TRACK RECORD WORKING WITH BOTH PRIVATE AND
PUBLIC LAND MANAGERS TO ADVOCATE AND STEWARD OUTDOOR CLIMBING
AREAS, MOST NOTABLY THE CHURCH OF JESUS CHRIST OF LATTER-DAY SAINTS IN
LITTLE COTTONWOOD CANYON, SNOWBIRD AND ALTA AT HELLGATE CLIFFS, AND
WITH THE UINTA WASATCH CACHE NATIONAL FOREST.

WE HAVE WORKED TO REDUCE CLIMBER IMPACTS AND INCREASE SAFETY FOR THE
PUBLIC AT OUTDOOR CLIMBING AREAS IN

AMERICAN FORK CANYON WITH THE REPLACEMENT OF FIXED ANCHORS AND
CLIMBING ACCESS TRAILS AND PLAN TO DO MORE IN UTAH COUNTY IN THE NEAR
FUTURE, HOPEFULLY WITH YOUR SUPPORT.

BRIDAL VEIL FALLS IS A CORNERSTONE OF UTAH ICE CLIMBING HISTORY (WHICH
IN TURN PLAYS A PROMINENT ROLE IN THE DEVELOPMENT OF ICE CLIMBING IN
THE GREATER U.S.). THE EXPERIENCE OF ICE CLIMBING AT BRIDAL VEIL IS
STEEPED IN CLIMBING HISTORY AND IS CHERISHED BY MANY. AMONG THE
EARLIEST RECORDED ASCENTS ARE GREG LOWE’S ASCENT OF BRIDAL VEIL IN 1971.
THE AREA DREW PROMINENT CLIMBERS, BOTH LOCALLY AND NATIONALLY,
THROUGHOUT THE LATE 1970S AND EARLY 808.

THE BRIDAL VEIL AREA REMAINS A PROMINENT CLIMBING DESTINATION TODAY.
THE UTAH ICE GUIDEBOOK LISTS 35 DISTINCT ICE CLIMBS IN THE AREA.

THE SLCA WOULD LIKE TO SEE LONG TERM ACCESS MAINTAINED WHILE
PROTECTING THIS NATURAL EXPERIENCE, WE SUPPORT A CONSERVATION
EASEMENT THAT ALLOWS FOR RECREATION AT BRIDAL VEIL FALLS AND WE HOPE
TO BE A STAKEHOLDER TO HELP STEWARD THIS RESOURCE INTO THE FUTURE,

THANK YOU FOR HEARING THE SALT LAKE CLIMBERS ALLIANCE INTERESTS IN THE
FUTURE OF BRIDAL VEIL FALLS.

WENDY PARMLEY, OREM - PLEASE PRESERVE BRIDAL VEIL FALLS FOR THE PUBLIC

ALEX GROW, PROVO - MY FAMILY PURCHASED BRIDAL VEIL FALLS IN 1974 FROM
THE CLEGG FAMILY AND OWNED AND OPERATED THE AERIAL TRAM AND SUPPORT
FACILITIES FROM THAT TIME UNTIL THEY MET THEIR DEMISE IN AN AVALANCHE
IN 1996, FROM 1996 TO 2015 WE OWNED THE PROPERTY UNTIL IT WAS SOLD TO UTAH
COUNTY IN HOPES THAT THE COUNTY WOULD BE MORE WELL POSITIONED TO
CREATE AN ENVIRONMENT WORTHY OF THIS NATURAL WONDER. I MET WITH
COMMISSIONERS YESTERDAY THAT INDICATED THE PROPOSED CONSERVATION
EASEMENT WASN'T EVEN FINALIZED. I FIND IT HIGHLY UNLIKELY THAT WE
UNDERSTAND TODAY EXACTLY WHAT IS BEING PROPOSED. THE EASEMENT IS
UNDENIABLY RUSHED. I WOULD PROPOSE THAT THE ITEM BE CONTINUED IN
ORDER TO GIVE COMMISSIONERS, STAFF, AND THE CONCERNED PUBLIC
SUFFICIENT TIME TO READ AND UNDERSTAND THE PROPOSED EASEMENT. MY
FAMILY WHEN OPERATING THE RESORT GRANTED A CONSERVATION EASEMENT
TO THE NATURE CONSERVANCY BASED IN WASHINGTON DC IN THE EARLY 908.
THERE'S NO WAY TO BE SURE THAT THE COUNTY EVEN OWNS THE RIGHTS THAT IS
PROPOSING TO DISPOSE OF TO A NONPROFIT PARTY THAT SUPPOSEDLY HAS MORE
OF THE INTEREST OF UTAH COUNTY RESIDENTS IN MIND THAN THEIR OWN
ELECTED GOVERNMENT. THIS PROCESS IS EXTREMELY RUSHED. PLEASE CONTINUE
THE ITEM SO ALLOW ALL PARTIES TO UNDERSTAND WHAT IS BEING PROPOSED
BETTER.

SOPHIE STEPHENS, OREM —1 AM STRONGLY AGAINST THE PRIVATIZATION OF
BRIDAL VEIL FALLS.

OTTO DE GROFF, HIGHLAND - AS A RESIDENT OF UTAH COUNTY FOR THE PAST 4
YEARS, I HAVE BEEN MANY TIMES TO THE AREA AROUND BRIDAL VEIL FALLS. THE
FALLS ARE A GREAT AREA TO GET OUTSIDE AND ENJOY NATURE. I AM GRATEFUL
TO HAVE AREAS LIKE THIS CLOSE TO UTAH VALLEY - BEING IN NATURE PROVIDES
ME WITH OPPORTUNITIES TO CALM ANXIETIES I HAVE, BREATHE CLEAN AIR, AND
FOSTER A BETTER CONNECTION WITH THE NON-HUMAN WORLD AROUND ME.
THESE THINGS, I FEEL, ARE ESSENTIAL COMPONENTS OF A GOOD, FULL LIFE.

BOARD OF COMMISSIONERS OF UTAH COUNTY, UTAH
Public Meeting Minutes

December 9, 2020

1, Pope

THE RECENT NEWS THAT THE FALLS AND SURROUNDING AREA MAY MOVE FROM A
PUBLIC TO PRIVATE SPACE HAS BEEN UNSETTLING. I KNOW THAT SOME YEARS
AGO A TRAM DID EXIST TO THE TOP OF THE FALLS, WITH A RESTAURANT, ETC. ON
TOP. BUT TODAY, I AM ADAMANT IN MY OPINION THAT THE AREA SURROUNDING
BRIDAL VEIL FALLS SHOULD BE PROTECTED AS A PUBLIC PLACE, UNDISTURBED BY
DEVELOPMENT AND DESIRES FOR MONETARY GAIN BY A FEW INDIVIDUALS.
PROTECTION OF THE FALLS WILL SHOW THAT UTAH COUNTY RESIDENTS VALUE
CLEAN AIR, OPEN SPACES, AND ACCESS TO OPEN, BEAUTIFUL PUBLIC SPACES OVER
INCREASED DEVELOPMENT OF OUR LIMITED NATURAL SPACES IN THE COUNTY. I
HOPE WE CAN ACT NOW TO KEEP BRIDAL VEIL FALLS THE WAY THEY ARE NOW: IN
THEIR NATURAL, BEAUTIFUL STATE.

NICKOLAS CLARKE, ALPINE - 1 AM WRITING IN REGARDS TO THE PROPOSAL TO
PLACE BRIDAL VEIL FALLS INTO A CONSERVATION EASEMENT VS
SELLING/LEASING THE PROPERTY FOR PRIVATE DEVELOPMENT. I AM STRONGLY
IN FAVOR OF PLACING THE FALLS AND THE SURROUNDING AREA INTO
CONSERVATION EASEMENT TO PREVENT PRIVATE DEVELOPMENT AND ENSURE
THE NATURAL AREAS PRESERVATION IN PERPETUITY FOR FUTURE GENERATIONS.

THE WASATCH RANGE, WHERE THESE FALLS ARE LOCATED, ARE BY DEFINITION A
LIMITED RESOURCE, THESE MOUNTAINS ARE ONE OF THE PRIMARY DRAWS FOR
MYSELF AND OTHERS TO LIVE AND WORK IN THE REGION. MUCH OF THE MOST
SPECTACULAR FEATURES OF THESE MOUNTAINS HAVE ALREADY BEEN SOLD OR
LEASED IN THE PAST TO PRIVATE INTERESTS. THERE IS IMMENSE PRESSURE ON
THE NATURAL AREAS ALONG THE WASATCH FRONT FOR A GROWING POPULATION
THAT IS INCREASINGLY INTERESTED IN ACCESS TO THE OUTDOORS, YEAR-ROUND.
PROVO CANYON, IN PARTICULAR, HAS INCREDIBLE PRESSURE, BUT SOME OF THE
MOST PRIVATE LAND HOLDINGS IN THE AREA. BRIDAL VEIL FALLS, WHICH
ALREADY HAS GREAT INFRASTRUCTURE FOR EASY ACCESS OF CITIZENS, HELP
RELIEVES PRESSURE FROM OTHER PARTS OF RANGE. FOR OUR LOCAL
GOVERNMENT TO SELL OFF OR OTHERWISE PRIVATIZE OUR PUBLIC LANDS THAT
CLEARLY ENJOY STRONG SUPPORT FROM LOCAL CITIZENS WOULD BE A TRAVESTY
AND SHORT-SIGHTED DECISION.

I SEE STRONG PARALLELS TO THE DISCUSSIONS HAD FURTHER NORTH IN THE
RANGE WHEN IT COMES TO EXPANSION AND INTERCONNECTION OF PRIVATE
LANDS HELD BY SKI RESORTS, AS WILL MCCARVILL, A LONG TIME WASATCH
BACKCOUNTRY USER HAS SAID: “WHEN PEOPLE TALK ABOUT COMPROMISE AND
SHARING, WHAT THEY FORGET IS THAT YEARS AGO THE PUBLIC MADE A MAJOR
COMPROMISE WHEN THEY GAVE AWAY ALL OF THE PRIME, UPPER ELEVATION,
NORTH FACING TERRAIN AT THE HEAD OF THE CANYONS TO THE SKI RESORTS.
WE’VE ALREADY MADE A HUGE COMPROMISE.”

THE SAME CAN BE SAID FOR BRIDAL VEIL FALLS AND THE PUBLIC LANDS IN PROVO
CANYON. THERE ARE ONLY SO MANY ACRES OF THE WASATCH RANGE THAT CAN
BE ENJOYED, THE PUBLIC HAS ALREADY MADE HUGE COMPROMISES, AND THERE IS
NO NEED TO PROVIDE MORE LAND FOR PRIVATE USE WHEN THE LOCAL
ECOSYSTEM IS ALREADY BEING STRETCHED TO SUPPORT THE RECREATIONAL USE
WE SEE TODAY. I URGE THE COMMISSION TO TAKE A LONG-TERM VIEW AND
PRESERVE THESE LANDS IN PERPETUITY.

WYATT GROW, OREM - MY NAME IS WYATT GROW AND DURING THE YEARS OF 1975-
2015, MY FAMILY OWNED THE BVF PROPERTY AND OPERATED THE BRIDAL VEIL
FALLS RESORT FROM 1975-1995. OVER THE COURSE OF YEARS, I'VE HEARD
INNUMERABLE STORIES FROM MEMBERS OF THE COMMUNITY RECOUNTING THEIR
CHERISHED MEMORIES OF BRIDAL VEIL FALLS. MARRIED COUPLES WHO MET AT
DANCES AT THE TOP LODGE, FORMER EMPLOYEES WHO SPENT THEIR SUMMERS
THERE, AND MANY OTHERS.

THE COMMENT THAT I WOULD LIKE TO GIVE IN THE CONVERSATION WE'RE
HAVING TODAY ABOUT BRIDAL VEIL FALLS BEING PLACED INTO A CONSERVATION
EASEMENT IS THIS: BRIDAL VEIL FALLS DESERVES AND NEEDS *SOME TYPE* OF
COMMERCIAL DEVELOPMENT AT THE SITE. THE NATURAL SPLENDOR OF BRIDAL
VEIL FALLS IS OVERWHELMING AND VERY WORTHY OF VIGILANT PROTECTION. AT
THE SAME TIME, THE MAJORITY OF THE TERRAIN AT BRIDAL VELL FALLS IS
EXTREMELY DIFFICULT TO TRAVERSE AND IS INACCESSIBLE TO MANY PEOPLE.
THE TRAM THAT ONCE CONNECTED THE BASE STATION TO THE TOP OF THE CLIFF
GAVE VISITORS A UNIQUE AND AWE-INSPIRING VIEW OF THE FALLS. I BELIEVE
THAT BRIDAL VEIL FALLS WOULD NOT BE THE LANDMARK THAT IT IS TODAY
WITHOUT ITS HISTORY AS A TRAM RESORT. I STILL VISIT BRIDAL VEIL FALLS WITH
MY FAMILY SEVERAL TIMES PER YEAR AND CAN SAY DEFINITIVELY THAT THERE
ARE FAR FEWER PEOPLE AT THE SITE THAN THERE WERE WHEN THE TRAM WAS
OPERATING.

PLEASE CONSIDER ALLOWING FOR REASONABLE *PUBLIC* DEVELOPMENT AT BVF
TO ENHANCE ITS ACCESSIBILITY AND ENJOYABILITY.
